local function func1(arg1)
	local tbl = {
		Url = "https://axer-chat-v2-default-rtdb.asia-southeast1.firebasedatabase.app/"
			.. arg1
			.. ".json?bust="
			.. tostring(os.clock()):gsub("%.", "")
			.. "_"
			.. math.random(1000, 9999),
		Method = "GET",
		Headers = { ["Cache-Control"] = "no-cache" },
	}

	local v1 = func(tbl)

	if v1 and (v1.StatusCode == 200 or v1.StatusCode == 304) and v1.Body and v1.Body ~= "null" then
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(v1.Body)
		end)
		return ok and typeof(result) == "table" and result or nil
	end

	return nil
end

	local function func17(arg1)
		return (arg1:gsub("..", function(arg1)
			return string.char(tonumber(arg1, 16) or 0)
		end))
	end

	math.randomseed(os.time() + tick())
	local v13 = bit32 and bit32.bxor
		or function(arg1, arg2)
			local num = 1
			local num1 = 0

			while arg1 > 0 or arg2 > 0 do
				if arg1 % 2 ~= arg2 % 2 then
					num1 = num1 + num
				end

				arg1 = math.floor(arg1 / 2)
				arg2 = math.floor(arg2 / 2)
				num = num * 2
			end

			return num1
		end

	local function func18(arg1)
		local v1 = string.upper(tostring(arg1)) .. "||" .. func17(v8())
		local num = 2166136261

		for i = 1, #v1 do
			num = v13(num, string.byte(v1, i)) * 16777619 % 4294967296
		end

		return string.format("%08X", num)
	end

	local function func19(arg1, arg2)
		local tbl = {}
		local num = 0

		for i = 0, 255 do
			tbl[i] = i
		end

		for i1 = 0, 255 do
			num = (num + tbl[i1] + string.byte(arg1, i1 % #arg1 + 1)) % 256
			local v1 = tbl[i1]
			tbl[i1] = tbl[num]
			tbl[num] = v1
		end

		local num1 = 0
		local tbl1 = {}
		local num2 = 0

		for i2 = 1, #arg2 do
			num1 = (num1 + 1) % 256
			num2 = (num2 + tbl[num1]) % 256
			local v2 = tbl[num1]
			tbl[num1] = tbl[num2]
			tbl[num2] = v2
			table.insert(tbl1, string.char(v13(string.byte(arg2, i2), tbl[(tbl[num1] + tbl[num2]) % 256])))
		end

		return table.concat(tbl1)
	end

	local function func20(arg1)
		return string.upper(arg1:gsub(".", function(arg1)
			return string.format("%02X", string.byte(arg1))
		end))
	end

	local function func21()
		local str = ""

		for _ = 1, 8 do
			local v1 = math.random(1, 62)
			str = str .. ("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789"):sub(v1, v1)
		end

		return str
	end

	local function func22(arg1, arg2)
		local v1 = arg1 .. "_TITAN_SECURE_VAULT_V14"
		local v2 = func21()
		local v3 = func20(func19(v1, v2 .. arg2))
		return v3, func18(v3)
	end

	local function func23(arg1, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg2 or not arg3 or func18(arg2) ~= string.upper(tostring(arg3)) then
			return nil
		end

		local ok, result = pcall(func17, arg2)

		if not ok or not result then
			return nil
		end

		local v1 = func19(arg1 .. "_TITAN_SECURE_VAULT_V14", result)
		return #v1 > 8 and v1:sub(9) or nil
	end

	end

	local function func26(arg1)
		if not arg1 then
			return ""
		end

		local _, _, v1 = arg1:match("^%[RPL::(.-)::(.-)::RPL%]%s*(.*)")
		local v2 = v1 or arg1

		if v2:sub(1, 5) == "[EDT]" then
			v2 = v2:sub(6)
		end

		if v2:match("^%[MEDIA::") then
			return "🖼️ [Image Vault]"
		end

		if v2:match("^%[STICKER://") then
			return "🏵️ [Sticker]"
		end

		return v2:gsub("<[^>]+>", "")
	end

	function tbl3.SyncNativeRobloxBubbleSettings()
		local v1 = tbl3.CurrentTheme == "Pink"
		local v2 = tbl3.CurrentTheme == "White"
		local color, color1, pcallRef

		if v2 then
			color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			if color then
				if v2 then
					color1 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
					if color1 then
						pcallRef = pcall
						pcallRef(function()
							local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
							if TextChatService and TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration") then
								TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.BackgroundColor3 = color
								TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.TextColor3 = color1
							end
						end)
						pcallRef(function()
							game:GetService("Chat")
								:SetBubbleChatSettings({ BackgroundColor3 = color, TextColor3 = color1 })
						end)
						return
					end
				end

				color1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				pcallRef = pcall
				pcallRef(function()
					local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
					if TextChatService and TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration") then
						TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.BackgroundColor3 = color
						TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.TextColor3 = color1
					end
				end)
				pcallRef(function()
					game:GetService("Chat"):SetBubbleChatSettings({ BackgroundColor3 = color, TextColor3 = color1 })
				end)
				return
			end
		end

		color = v1 and Color3.fromRGB(30, 15, 26) or Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)

		if v2 then
			color1 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
			if color1 then
				pcallRef = pcall
				pcallRef(function()
					local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
					if TextChatService and TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration") then
						TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.BackgroundColor3 = color
						TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.TextColor3 = color1
					end
				end)
				pcallRef(function()
					game:GetService("Chat"):SetBubbleChatSettings({ BackgroundColor3 = color, TextColor3 = color1 })
				end)
				return
			end
		end

		color1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		pcallRef = pcall
		pcallRef(function()
			local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
			if TextChatService and TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration") then
				TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.BackgroundColor3 = color
				TextChatService.BubbleChatConfiguration.TextColor3 = color1
			end
		end)
		pcallRef(function()
			game:GetService("Chat"):SetBubbleChatSettings({ BackgroundColor3 = color, TextColor3 = color1 })
		end)
	end

	function tbl3.SetLastRead(arg1, arg2)

	function tbl3.GetUnreadCount(arg1)
		local num = 0
		local v1 = tbl3.GetLastRead(arg1)

		for _, v2 in ipairs(tbl3.Cache.DMs[arg1] or {}) do
			if v2.Time > v1 and v2.UserID ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then
				num = num + 1
			end
		end

		return num
	end

	function tbl3.RegisterAccount(arg1, arg2, arg3)
		if not arg1 or typeof(arg1) ~= "string" or arg1 == "" then
			return
		end
		tbl3.AccountRegistry[arg1:lower()] = {
			Username = arg1,
			DisplayName = arg2 or arg1,
			UserId = arg3 or 1,
		}
	end

	function tbl3.GetAccount(arg1)
		if not arg1 or typeof(arg1) ~= "string" then
			return { Username = "Unknown", DisplayName = "Unknown", UserId = 1 }
		end

		local v1 = arg1:lower()

		if tbl3.AccountRegistry[v1] then
			return tbl3.AccountRegistry[v1]
		end

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if player.Name:lower() == v1 then
				tbl3.RegisterAccount(player.Name, player.DisplayName, player.UserId)
				return tbl3.AccountRegistry[v1]
			end
		end

		return { Username = arg1, DisplayName = arg1, UserId = 1 }
	end

	local function func31(arg1, image)
		if not image then
			return
		end

		if image:sub(1, 10):lower() == "rbxassetid" or image:sub(1, 9):lower() == "rbxthumb:" then
			arg1.Image = image
			return
		end

		if not getcustomasset or not writefile or not isfile then
			return
		end

		local v1 = "criticalMediaCache_" .. func18(image) .. ".png"

		if pcall(isfile, v1) and isfile(v1) then
			pcall(function()
				arg1.Image = getcustomasset(v1)
			end)
			return
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return v3({ Url = image, Method = "GET" })
			end)
			if ok and result and result.StatusCode == 200 and result.Body then
				pcall(function()
					writefile(v1, result.Body)
					arg1.Image = getcustomasset(v1)
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function func32(arg1, name)
		if name == "ARIA" then
			arg1.Image = "rbxassetid://92917449577250"
			return
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			local v1 = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(name)
			end)
			arg1.Image = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
				v1 and v1.UserId or (ok and result or 1),
				Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
				Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48
			)
		end)
	end

	function tbl3.DispatchGodCommand(arg1)
		if not func12() then
			func11("GOD-SPOOF", "Illegal God Execution")
			return
		end
		if arg1:sub(1, 9) == "BAN_USER:" then
			func3("Blacklist/Accounts/" .. arg1:sub(10):lower(), "true", true)
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 11) == "UNBAN_USER:" then
			local v1 = arg1:sub(12):lower()
			task.spawn(function()
				local v2 = func5("Blacklist/Accounts/" .. v1)

				if v2 and type(v2) == "string" and v2 ~= "null" and v2 ~= "true" then
					func4("Blacklist/HWIDs/" .. v2:gsub('"', ""))
				end

				func4("Blacklist/Accounts/" .. v1)
			end)
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 10) == "LOCK_CHAT:" then
			func2("Locks/" .. arg1:sub(11):lower(), "true", true)
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 12) == "UNLOCK_CHAT:" then
			func4("Locks/" .. arg1:sub(13):lower())
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 13) == "MUTE_KEYWORD:" then
			func2("Mutes/Keywords/" .. arg1:sub(14):lower(), "true", true)
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 15) == "UNMUTE_KEYWORD:" then
			func4("Mutes/Keywords/" .. arg1:sub(16):lower())
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 10) == "GRANT_VIP:" then
			local v2 = arg1:sub(11):lower()
			tbl3.VIPList[v2] = true
			func2("VIP/" .. v2, "true", true)
			tbl3.RefreshAllVisibleVIPBadges()
			tbl3.TriggerToast("👑 VIP Granted", "@" .. v2 .. " is now a VIP!", false)
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 12) == "REVOKE_VIP:" then
			local v3 = arg1:sub(13):lower()
			tbl3.VIPList[v3] = nil
			func4("VIP/" .. v3)
			tbl3.RefreshAllVisibleVIPBadges()
			tbl3.TriggerToast("👑 VIP Revoked", "@" .. v3 .. " is no longer VIP.", false)
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 12) == "GRANT_TITLE:" then
			local v4, v5 = arg1:sub(13):match("^([^:]+):(.+)$")
			if v4 and v5 then
				local v6 = v4:lower()
				tbl3.TitleRegistry[v6] = v5
				func2("Titles/" .. v6, v5, true)
				tbl3.LiveSyncAllVisibleBubblesTitle()
			end
		elseif arg1:sub(1, 13) == "REVOKE_TITLE:" then
			local v7 = arg1:sub(14):lower()
			tbl3.TitleRegistry[v7] = nil
			func4("Titles/" .. v7)
			tbl3.LiveSyncAllVisibleBubblesTitle()
		end
	end

	function tbl3.SetLockState(isLocked)
		tbl3.IsLocked = isLocked

		if not tbl3.UI.TextBox then
			return
		end

		if isLocked then
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.TextEditable = false
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.Text = ""
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.PlaceholderText = "🔒 CHAT LOCKED BY ADMIN"
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
			tbl3.UI.SendBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 20, 20)
		else
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.TextEditable = true
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.PlaceholderText = "Type message..."
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.TextColor3 = tbl4[tbl3.CurrentTheme].TextColor
			tbl3.UI.SendBtn.BackgroundColor3 = tbl4[tbl3.CurrentTheme].Accent
		end
	end

	function tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal(arg1)

			{ Sender = "ARIA", UserID = 0, Msg = arg1, Time = os.time(), ID = v1, Edited = false }
		)
		tbl3.RenderBubble("ARIA", 0, arg1, os.time(), v1, false)
		tbl3.HandleScroll(true)
	end

	local function func34()
		task.spawn(function()
			local v1 = func1("Announcements")
			local v2

			if type(v1) == "table" then
				local tbl = {}

				for k in pairs(v1) do
					local insert = table.insert
					v2 = tbl
					insert(v2, k)
				end

				table.sort(tbl)

				if #tbl > 0 then
					str = tbl[#tbl]
				end
			end

			while task.wait(4) do
				if getgenv().criticalChat_Unloading then
					break
				end

				local v3 = func1("Announcements")

				if type(v3) == "table" then
					local tbl1 = {}

					for k1 in pairs(v3) do
						v2 = table.insert
						v2(tbl1, k1)
					end

					table.sort(tbl1)

					for _, v4 in ipairs(tbl1) do
						v2 = str
						if v4 > v2 then
							str = v4
							v2 = v3[v4]

							if v2 and v2.text and v2.sender ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
								task.spawn(function()
									tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal(
										"📢 <b>CROSS-SERVER ANNOUNCEMENT</b>\n<font color='#B43CFF'>From: @"
											.. tostring(v2.sender)
											.. "</font>\n<font color='#888888'>─────────────────</font>\n\n"
											.. tostring(v2.text)
											.. "\n\n<font color='#888888'>─────────────────</font>"
									)
									task.wait(0.8)
									tbl3.TriggerToast("📢 Announcement", tostring(v2.text):sub(1, 60), false)
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function func35()
		task.spawn(function()
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://135244211779631"
			sound.Volume = 1.5
			sound.Parent = v2 or game:GetService("CoreGui")
			sound:Play()
			sound.Ended:Connect(function()
				sound:Destroy()
			end)
		end)
	end

	local function func36(arg1, arg2)
		if not arg1 then
			return
		end

		if arg1.Visible then
			return
		end

		local v1 = arg1:GetAttribute("criticalBaseY") or arg1.Position.Y.Offset
		arg1:SetAttribute("criticalBaseY", v1)
		local v2 = arg2 or v1 + 30
		arg1.Position = UDim2.new(arg1.Position.X.Scale, arg1.Position.X.Offset, arg1.Position.Y.Scale, v2)
		arg1.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		if arg1:FindFirstChild("UIStroke") then
			arg1.UIStroke.Transparency = 1
		end

		arg1.Visible = true
		TweenService:Create(arg1, TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = UDim2.new(arg1.Position.X.Scale, arg1.Position.X.Offset, arg1.Position.Y.Scale, v1),
			BackgroundTransparency = tbl4[tbl3.CurrentTheme].HeadTrans,
		}):Play()

		if arg1:FindFirstChild("UIStroke") then
			TweenService:Create(arg1.UIStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.32), { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
		end
	end

	local function func37(arg1)
		if not arg1 or not arg1.Visible then
			return
		end

		local v1 = arg1:GetAttribute("criticalBaseY") or arg1.Position.Y.Offset
		arg1:SetAttribute("criticalBaseY", v1)
		local tween = TweenService:Create(arg1, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
			Position = UDim2.new(arg1.Position.X.Scale, arg1.Position.X.Offset, arg1.Position.Y.Scale, v1 + 25),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		})

		if arg1:FindFirstChild("UIStroke") then
			TweenService:Create(arg1.UIStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.22), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
		end

		tween:Play()
		tween.Completed:Connect(function()
			if arg1 then
				arg1.Visible = false
				arg1.Position = UDim2.new(arg1.Position.X.Scale, arg1.Position.X.Offset, arg1.Position.Y.Scale, v1)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function func38()
		func35()
		local MainBg = tbl3.UI.MainBg

		if not MainBg then
			return
		end

		if MainBg.Visible then
			if tbl3.UI.StickerTray then
				func37(tbl3.UI.StickerTray)
			end

			if tbl3.UI.ImageTray then
				func37(tbl3.UI.ImageTray)
			end

			if tbl3.UI.AdminTray then
				func37(tbl3.UI.AdminTray)
			end

			tbl3.CancelActionBar()
			MainBg.Visible = false
		else
			MainBg.Visible = true
		end
	end

	local function func39(arg1)
		if not arg1 or not arg1.Parent then
			return
		end

		local Parent = arg1.Parent
		local criticalCurvedTitleArc = Parent:FindFirstChild("criticalCurvedTitleArc")

		if criticalCurvedTitleArc then
			criticalCurvedTitleArc:Destroy()
		end

		local criticalCurvedTitleArc = Instance.new("Frame", Parent)
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.Name = "criticalCurvedTitleArc"
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.Size = arg1.Size
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.Position = arg1.Position
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.AnchorPoint = arg1.AnchorPoint
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.ZIndex = arg1.ZIndex + 5
		local v1 = arg1.Size.X.Offset / 2 + 3.5
		local v2 = arg1.Size.X.Offset / 2
		local v3 = arg1.Size.Y.Offset / 2
		local tbl = {}

		for i = 1, 3 do
			local v4 = -6187.944187412891 + (i - 1) * 2062.648062470963 / 2 or -6187.944187412891
			local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", criticalCurvedTitleArc)
			textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
			textLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, v2 + math.cos(v4) * v1, 0, v3 + math.sin(v4) * v1)
			textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			textLabel.Text = ("VIP"):sub(i, i)
			textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			textLabel.TextSize = 8.5
			textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
			textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
			textLabel.Rotation = math.deg(v4) + 90
			local uiGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", textLabel)
			uiGradient.Rotation = 45
			uiGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
				ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)),
				ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0)),
				ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)),
			})
			table.insert(tbl, { l = textLabel, idx = i })
		end

		local connection
		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not criticalCurvedTitleArc or not criticalCurvedTitleArc.Parent or getgenv().criticalChat_Unloading then
				connection:Disconnect()
				return
			end

			local v1 = os.clock()

			for _, v2 in ipairs(tbl) do
				local v3 = (v1 - v2.idx * 0.22) % 10
				v2.l.TextTransparency = v3 < 5 and 0
					or (
						v3 < 6 and (1 - math.cos((v3 - 5) * math.pi)) / 2
						or (v3 < 9 and 1 or (1 + math.cos((v3 - 9) * math.pi)) / 2)
					)
			end
		end)
	end

	function tbl3.RefreshAllVisibleVIPBadges()
		for _, child in pairs(tbl3.UI.Scroll:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Frame") and child:FindFirstChild("MessageBubble") then
				local MessageBubble = child.MessageBubble
				local ImageLabel = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageLabel")

				if ImageLabel then
					local str = ""
					local SenderName = MessageBubble:FindFirstChild("SenderName")

					if SenderName then
						str = SenderName.Text
					elseif MessageBubble.AnchorPoint.X == 1 then
						str = LocalPlayer.Name
					end

					local v1 = str:lower()

					if ImageLabel.Parent:FindFirstChild("criticalCurvedTitleArc") then
						ImageLabel.Parent.criticalCurvedTitleArc:Destroy()
					end

					if tbl3.VIPList[v1] then
						func39(ImageLabel)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function tbl3.RefreshVaultUI()
		local VaultScroll = tbl3.UI.ImageTray:FindFirstChild("VaultScroll")

		if not VaultScroll then
			return
		end

		for _, child in pairs(VaultScroll:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("ImageButton") or child:IsA("TextLabel") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end

		local v1 = func27()
		local v2 = func29()
		local FavoritesMode = tbl3.FavoritesMode
		local v3 = tbl4[tbl3.CurrentTheme]
		local tbl = {}

		for i, v4 in ipairs(v1) do
			if FavoritesMode then
				if v2[v4] then
					table.insert(tbl, { url = v4, origIdx = i })
				end
			else
				table.insert(tbl, { url = v4, origIdx = i })
			end
		end

		local v5 = #tbl

		if v5 == 0 then
			local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", VaultScroll)
			textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -8)
			textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 4)
			textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
			textLabel.TextSize = 11
			textLabel.TextColor3 = v3.SubText
			textLabel.ZIndex = 72
			textLabel.RichText = true

			if FavoritesMode then
				textLabel.Text =
					"⭐ No favorites yet!\n\nTap the ⭐ star on any image\nto add it here for quick access.\n\n<font color='#B43CFF'>👉 Type <b>/helpphoto</b> for guide</font>"
			else
				textLabel.Text =
					"📂 Vault is empty!\n\nPaste an image URL above\nand click <b>Save</b> to store it.\n\n<font color='#B43CFF'>👉 Type <b>/helpphoto</b> for guide</font>"
			end

			VaultScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 130)
			return
		end

		VaultScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(140, math.ceil(v5 / 5) * 70 + 20))

		for _, v6 in ipairs(tbl) do
			local url = v6.url
			local imageButton = Instance.new("ImageButton", VaultScroll)
			imageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 62)
			imageButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 35)
			imageButton.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
			imageButton.LayoutOrder = v6.origIdx
			imageButton.ZIndex = 72
			imageButton.ClipsDescendants = false
			Instance.new("UICorner", imageButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
			Instance.new("UIStroke", imageButton).Color = v2[url] and Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0) or v3.StrokeColor
			func31(imageButton, url)
			local v7 = v2[url] and true or false
			local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton", imageButton)
			textButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
			textButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, -4, 0, -4)
			textButton.BackgroundColor3 = v7 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
			textButton.BackgroundTransparency = v7 and 0.1 or 0.4
			textButton.Text = v7 and "⭐" or "☆"
			textButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			textButton.TextSize = 12
			textButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			textButton.ZIndex = 74
			textButton.AutoButtonColor = false
			Instance.new("UICorner", textButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
			textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local v1 = func29()

				if v1[url] then
					v1[url] = nil
					tbl3.TriggerToast("⭐ Removed", "Removed from favorites", false)
				else
					v1[url] = true
					tbl3.TriggerToast("⭐ Favorited!", "Added to your favorites", false)
				end

				func30(v1)
				tbl3.RefreshVaultUI()
			end)
			local textButton1 = Instance.new("TextButton", imageButton)
			textButton1.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
			textButton1.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, -3)
			textButton1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 45, 45)
			textButton1.Text = "X"
			textButton1.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			textButton1.TextSize = 10
			textButton1.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			textButton1.ZIndex = 74
			Instance.new("UICorner", textButton1).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
			textButton1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local v1 = func27()
				local v2 = func29()
				local v3 = #v1

				for i = v3, 1, -1 do
					if v1[i] == url then
						table.remove(v1, v3)
						break
					end
				end

				v2[url] = nil
				func28(v1)
				func30(v2)
				tbl3.RefreshVaultUI()
				tbl3.TriggerToast("🗑️ Deleted", "Image removed from vault", false)
			end)
			imageButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				tbl3.Send("[MEDIA::" .. url .. "::MEDIA]")
				func37(tbl3.UI.ImageTray)
			end)
		end
	end

	function tbl3.DressInboxCard(arg1, _, arg3, arg4)
		local v1 = tbl4[tbl3.CurrentTheme]
		local Sub = arg1:FindFirstChild("Sub")

		if Sub then
			local v2 = func26(arg3)
			Sub.Text = v2:sub(1, 35) .. (#v2 > 35 and "..." or "")
		end

		local criticalUnreadBadge = arg1:FindFirstChild("criticalUnreadBadge")

		if arg4 > 0 then
			if not criticalUnreadBadge then
				local frame = Instance.new("Frame", arg1)
				criticalUnreadBadge = frame
				frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 22)
				frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0.5, -11)
				frame.BackgroundColor3 = v1.Accent
				Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
				local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
				textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
				textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				textLabel.TextSize = 10
				textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			end

			criticalUnreadBadge.Txt.Text = arg4 >= 4 and "4+ New Msgs" or tostring(arg4) .. " New Msg"
			arg1.BackgroundColor3 = v1.Accent
			arg1.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85
		else
			if criticalUnreadBadge then
				criticalUnreadBadge:Destroy()
			end

			arg1.BackgroundColor3 = v1.OtherBubble
			arg1.BackgroundTransparency = v1.BubOtherTrans
		end
	end

	function tbl3.TriggerToast(arg1, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
		local v1 = tbl4[tbl3.CurrentTheme]
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 54)
		frame.BackgroundColor3 = v1.HeaderBg
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0)
		frame.ZIndex = 200
		Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
		local uiStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
		uiStroke.Color = arg3 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 220, 115) or v1.Accent
		uiStroke.Transparency = 1
		uiStroke.Thickness = 1.2
		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
		textLabel.TextSize = 12
		textLabel.TextColor3 = v1.TextColor
		textLabel.TextTransparency = 1
		textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		textLabel.RichText = true
		textLabel.Text = string.format("<b>%s</b>\n<font color='#888888'>%s</font>", arg1, func26(arg2):sub(1, 30))
		local imageLabel

		if arg5 then
			textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 1, 0)
			textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 0)
			imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel", frame)
			imageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 34)
			imageLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -17)
			imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			imageLabel.ImageTransparency = 1
			Instance.new("UICorner", imageLabel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
			func32(imageLabel, arg5)
		end

		frame.Parent = criticalSmartToastContainer
		TweenService:Create(
			frame,
			TweenInfo.new(0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{ Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = v1.HeadTrans }
		):Play()
		TweenService
			:Create(
				uiStroke,
				TweenInfo.new(0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
				{ Transparency = 0.15 }
			)
			:Play()
		TweenService:Create(textLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()

		if imageLabel then
			TweenService:Create(imageLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), { ImageTransparency = 0 })
				:Play()
		end

		if arg3 and arg4 then
			local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
			textButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			textButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			textButton.Text = ""
			textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				tbl3.SwitchMode("DM", arg4)

				if not tbl3.UI.MainBg.Visible then
					tbl3.UI.MainBg.Visible = true
				end

				frame:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(4)
			if frame.Parent then
				local tween = TweenService:Create(
					frame,
					TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
					{ Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }
				)
				TweenService:Create(uiStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
				TweenService:Create(textLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()

				if imageLabel then
					TweenService:Create(imageLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { ImageTransparency = 1 }):Play()
				end

				tween:Play()
				tween.Completed:Wait()
				frame:Destroy()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function func40(arg1, arg2)
		if not arg1 or not arg1.Parent then
			return
		end

		local Parent = arg1.Parent
		local criticalCurvedTitleArc = Parent:FindFirstChild("criticalCurvedTitleArc")

		if criticalCurvedTitleArc then
			criticalCurvedTitleArc:Destroy()
		end

		local criticalCurvedTitleArc = Instance.new("Frame")
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.Name = "criticalCurvedTitleArc"
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.Size = arg1.Size
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.Position = arg1.Position
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.AnchorPoint = arg1.AnchorPoint
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.ZIndex = arg1.ZIndex + 5
		criticalCurvedTitleArc.Parent = Parent
		local v1 = #arg2
		local v2 = arg1.Size.X.Offset / 2 + 3.5
		local v3 = math.rad(-90 - math.min((v1 - 1) * 18, 160) / 2)
		local v4 = math.rad(-90 + math.min((v1 - 1) * 18, 160) / 2)
		local v5 = arg1.Size.X.Offset / 2
		local v6 = arg1.Size.Y.Offset / 2
		local tbl = {}

		for i = 1, v1 do
			local v7 = v1 > 1 and v3 + (i - 1) * (v4 - v3) / (v1 - 1) or v3
			local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", criticalCurvedTitleArc)
			textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
			textLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, v5 + math.cos(v7) * v2, 0, v6 + math.sin(v7) * v2)
			textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			textLabel.Text = arg2:sub(i, i)
			textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			textLabel.TextSize = 8.5
			textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
			textLabel.Rotation = math.deg(v7) + 90
			local uiGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", textLabel)
			uiGradient.Rotation = 45
			uiGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
				ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 195, 255)),
				ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 255)),
			})
			table.insert(tbl, { l = textLabel, idx = i })
		end

		local connection
		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not criticalCurvedTitleArc or not criticalCurvedTitleArc.Parent or getgenv().criticalChat_Unloading then
				connection:Disconnect()
				return
			end

			local v1 = os.clock()

			for _, v2 in ipairs(tbl) do
				local v3 = (v1 - v2.idx * 0.22) % 10
				v2.l.TextTransparency = v3 < 5 and 0
					or (
						v3 < 6 and (1 - math.cos((v3 - 5) * math.pi)) / 2
						or (v3 < 9 and 1 or (1 + math.cos((v3 - 9) * math.pi)) / 2)
					)
			end
		end)
	end

	function tbl3.LiveSyncAllVisibleBubblesTitle()
		for _, child in pairs(tbl3.UI.Scroll:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Frame") and child:FindFirstChild("MessageBubble") then
				local ImageLabel = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageLabel")
				local MessageBubble = child.MessageBubble

				if ImageLabel and MessageBubble then
					local str = ""
					local SenderName = MessageBubble:FindFirstChild("SenderName")

					if SenderName then
						str = SenderName.Text
					elseif MessageBubble.AnchorPoint.X == 1 then
						str = LocalPlayer.Name
					end

					local v1 = tbl3.TitleRegistry[str:lower()]
						or (MessageBubble.AnchorPoint.X == 1 and LocalPlayer.UserId == 7169032620 and "CREATOR" or nil)

					if v1 then
						func40(ImageLabel, v1)
					elseif ImageLabel.Parent:FindFirstChild("criticalCurvedTitleArc") then
						ImageLabel.Parent.criticalCurvedTitleArc:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	function tbl3.RenderBubble(arg1, arg2, arg3, _, arg5, arg6)
		local v1 = tbl4[tbl5[num]]
		local v2 = string.lower(arg1) == string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name)
		local v3 = arg1 == "ARIA"
		local v4, v5, v6 = arg3:match("^%[RPL::(.-)::(.-)::RPL%]%s*(.*)")
		local v7 = v6

		if not v4 then
			v7 = arg3
		end

		local v8 = arg6 or false

		if v7:sub(1, 5) == "[EDT]" then
			v8 = true
			v7 = v7:sub(6)
		end

		local frame = Instance.new("Frame", tbl3.UI.Scroll)
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		if arg5 then
			tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[arg5] = frame
		end

		local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel", frame)
		local uDim2, uDim21, v9, v10, v11, uDim22, textLabel, uDim23, frame1, uiListLayout, uiPadding, v12, textLabel1, enum, v13, frame2, uiPadding1, frame3, textLabel2, enum1, v14, v15, flag, imageLabel1, frame4, imageButton, uiStroke, textLabel3, enum2, v16, MainMessageText, SenderName, flag1, flag2, num1

		if v3 then
			uDim2 = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32)
			if uDim2 then
				imageLabel.Size = uDim2
				imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1

	function tbl3.OpenActionMenu(arg1, _, arg3, arg4, arg5)
		if arg1 == "ARIA" or tbl3.UI.MainBg:FindFirstChild("criticalHoldActionMenu") then
			return
		end

		local DisplayName = tbl3.GetAccount(arg1).DisplayName
		local v1 = arg3:sub(1, 5) == "[EDT]" and arg3:sub(6) or arg3
		local v2 = tbl4[tbl5[num]]
		local criticalHoldActionMenu = Instance.new("Frame", tbl3.UI.MainBg)
		criticalHoldActionMenu.Name = "criticalHoldActionMenu"
		criticalHoldActionMenu.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		criticalHoldActionMenu.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
		criticalHoldActionMenu.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		criticalHoldActionMenu.Active = true
		criticalHoldActionMenu.ZIndex = 85
		local v3 = v1:match("^%[STICKER://") or v1:match("^%[MEDIA::")
		local num1 = 3

		if arg5 and arg4 then
			num1 = 3 + (v3 and 1 or 2)
		end

		local frame = Instance.new("Frame", criticalHoldActionMenu)
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 75 + num1 * 34)
		frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		frame.BackgroundColor3 = v2.HeaderBg
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = v2.HeadTrans
		frame.ZIndex = 86
		frame.ClipsDescendants = true
		Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
		Instance.new("UIStroke", frame).Color = v2.Accent
		local uiListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", frame)
		uiListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
		uiListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
		uiListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		Instance.new("UIPadding", frame).PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10)
		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textLabel.TextSize = 12
		textLabel.TextColor3 = v2.SubText
		textLabel.LayoutOrder = 1
		textLabel.ZIndex = 87
		textLabel.Text = "Options (" .. DisplayName .. ")"
		local v4 = func26(v1)
		local textLabel1 = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
		textLabel1.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 32)
		textLabel1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel1.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
		textLabel1.TextSize = 11
		textLabel1.TextColor3 = v2.TextColor
		textLabel1.TextWrapped = true
		textLabel1.LayoutOrder = 2
		textLabel1.ZIndex = 87
		textLabel1.Text = v4 == v1 and '"' .. v1:sub(1, 36) .. '..."' or v4
		local num2 = 3

		local function func(arg1, arg2, backgroundColor3, arg4)
			local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
			textButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 30)
			textButton.BackgroundColor3 = backgroundColor3
			textButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
			textButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			textButton.TextSize = 12
			textButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			textButton.LayoutOrder = num2
			textButton.ZIndex = 87
			textButton.Text = arg2 .. " " .. arg1
			Instance.new("UICorner", textButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
			textButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				arg4()
				criticalHoldActionMenu:Destroy()
			end)
			num2 = num2 + 1
		end

		func("Copy Text", "📋", Color3.fromRGB(35, 110, 200), function()
			local v2 = setclipboard or toclipboard
			if v2 then
				v2(v1)
				tbl3.TriggerToast("📋 Copied", "Text saved", false)
			end
		end)

		if arg5 and arg4 and not v3 then
			func("Edit Message", "✏️", Color3.fromRGB(210, 130, 30), function()
				tbl3.StartEditing(arg4, v1)
			end)
		end

		func("Reply", "↩️", v2.Accent, function()
			tbl3.SetReply(arg1, v1, DisplayName)
		end)

		if arg5 and arg4 then
			func("Unsend", "🗑️", Color3.fromRGB(210, 45, 45), function()
				tbl3.UnsendMsg(arg4)
			end)
		end

		func("Cancel", "X", Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70), function() end)
		TweenService:Create(criticalHoldActionMenu, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 }):Play()
	end

	function tbl3.UnsendMsg(arg1)
		local v1 = LocalPlayer.Name:lower()
		local v2 = tbl3.ActiveDM and tbl3.ActiveDM:lower() or ""
		task.spawn(function()
			if tbl3.Mode == "DM" and v2 ~= "" then
				func4("DMs/" .. v1 .. "/" .. v2 .. "/" .. arg1)
				func4("DMs/" .. v2 .. "/" .. v1 .. "/" .. arg1)
			else
				func4("servers/" .. v10 .. "/chat/" .. arg1)
			end
		end)
		tbl3.TriggerToast("🗑️ Unsent", "Message recalled globally", false)

		if tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[arg1] then
			tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[arg1]:Destroy()
			tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[arg1] = nil
		end
	end

	function tbl3.StartEditing(editingMsgID, text)
		tbl3.EditingMsgID = editingMsgID
		tbl3.UI.TextBox.Text = text
		tbl3.UI.ReplyIcon.Text = "✏️"
		tbl3.UI.ReplyText.Text = "Editing your message...\n" .. text:sub(1, 28)
		tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview.Visible = true
		TweenService:Create(
			tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview,
			TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{ Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -84) }
		):Play()
		tbl3.UI.TextBox:CaptureFocus()
	end

	function tbl3.SetReply(arg1, arg2, arg3)
		local v1 = func26(arg2)

		if #v1 > 28 and not v1:match("^%[") then
			v1 = v1:sub(1, 28) .. "..."
		end

		tbl3.ReplyingTo = { Username = arg1, DispName = arg3, Text = v1 }
		tbl3.UI.ReplyText.Text = "Replying to <b>" .. arg3 .. "</b>\n" .. v1
		tbl3.UI.ReplyIcon.Text = "↩"
		tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview.Visible = true
		TweenService:Create(
			tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview,
			TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{ Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -84) }
		):Play()
		tbl3.UI.TextBox:CaptureFocus()
	end

	function tbl3.CancelActionBar()
		tbl3.ReplyingTo = nil
		tbl3.EditingMsgID = nil
		local tween = TweenService:Create(
			tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview,
			TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{ Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -46) }
		)
		tween:Play()
		task.spawn(function()
			tween.Completed:Wait()
			tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview.Visible = false
		end)
	end

	function tbl3.HandleScroll(arg1)
		task.spawn(function()
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
			local v1 = tbl3.UI.Scroll.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - tbl3.UI.Scroll.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y

			if v1 <= 0 then
				return
			end

			local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.38, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

			if arg1 then
				TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.Scroll, tweenInfo, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, v1) }):Play()
				tbl3.UI.JumpBtn.Visible = false
			elseif tbl3.UI.Scroll.CanvasPosition.Y < v1 - 50 then
				tbl3.UI.JumpBtn.Visible = true
			else
				TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.Scroll, tweenInfo, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, v1) }):Play()
			end
		end)
	end

	function tbl3.LiveUpdateInboxSort()
		if tbl3.Mode ~= "DM_Inbox" then
			return
		end

		local tbl = {}

		for k, v1 in pairs(tbl3.Cache.DMs) do
			table.insert(tbl, {
				k = k,
				t = v1[#v1] and v1[#v1].Time or 0,
				s = v1[#v1] and v1[#v1].Msg or "Start secure chat...",
				u = tbl3.GetUnreadCount(k),
			})
		end

		table.sort(tbl, function(a, b)
			return a.t > b.t
		end)

		for i, v2 in ipairs(tbl) do
			local v3 = tbl3.UI.Scroll:FindFirstChild("DM_Card_" .. v2.k)
			if v3 then
				v3.LayoutOrder = i
				tbl3.DressInboxCard(v3, v2.k, v2.s, v2.u)
			else
				local v4 = tbl3.GetAccount(v2.k)
				tbl3.RenderInboxCard(v4.DisplayName, v4.Username, v2.k, v2.s)
			end
		end
	end

	function tbl3.SwitchMode(mode, arg2)
		tbl3.Mode = mode
		tbl3.ActiveDM = arg2 and arg2:lower() or nil

		for _, child in pairs(tbl3.UI.Scroll:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end

		tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes = {}
		tbl3.MessagesCount = 0

		if mode == "Server" then
			tbl3.UI.HeaderDP.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.Title.Text = "critical CHAT"
			tbl3.UI.Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 0)
			tbl3.UI.Back.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.InboxBtn.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.MuteBtn.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.SearchWrapper.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.SearchInput.Text = ""
			tbl3.UI.InputWrapper.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.StickerBtn.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.ImageBtn.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.Scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 34)
			tbl3.UI.Scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -80)
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.PlaceholderText = tbl3.IsLocked and "🔒 CHAT LOCKED BY ADMIN" or "Type message..."

			for _, v1 in pairs(tbl3.Cache.Server) do
				tbl3.RenderBubble(v1.Sender, v1.UserID, v1.Msg, v1.Time, v1.ID, v1.Edited)
			end
		elseif mode == "DM_Inbox" then
			tbl3.UI.HeaderDP.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.Title.Text = "DM Inbox"
			tbl3.UI.Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 0)
			tbl3.UI.Back.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.InboxBtn.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.MuteBtn.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.InputWrapper.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.StickerTray.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.ImageTray.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.SearchWrapper.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.SearchWrapper.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 40)
			tbl3.UI.Scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 82)
			tbl3.UI.Scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -88)
			local tbl = {}

			for k, v2 in pairs(tbl3.Cache.DMs) do
				local v3 = tbl3.GetAccount(k)
				table.insert(tbl, {
					k = k,
					d = v3.DisplayName,
					u = v3.Username,
					s = v2[#v2] and v2[#v2].Msg or "Tap to chat...",
					t = v2[#v2] and v2[#v2].Time or 0,
				})
			end

			table.sort(tbl, function(a, b)
				return a.t > b.t
			end)

			for i, v4 in ipairs(tbl) do
				tbl3.RenderInboxCard(v4.d, v4.u, v4.k, v4.s)
				local v5 = tbl3.UI.Scroll:FindFirstChild("DM_Card_" .. v4.k)

				if v5 then
					v5.LayoutOrder = i
				end
			end
		elseif mode == "DM" then
			tbl3.SetLastRead(arg2, (func16()))
			local v6 = tbl3.GetAccount(arg2)
			tbl3.UI.HeaderDP.Visible = true
			func32(tbl3.UI.HeaderDP, v6.Username)
			tbl3.UI.Title.Text = v6.DisplayName .. " (@" .. v6.Username .. ")"
			tbl3.UI.Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 68, 0, 0)
			tbl3.UI.Back.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.InboxBtn.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.MuteBtn.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.SearchWrapper.Visible = false
			tbl3.UI.InputWrapper.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.StickerBtn.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.ImageBtn.Visible = true
			tbl3.UI.Scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 34)
			tbl3.UI.Scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -80)
			tbl3.UI.TextBox.PlaceholderText = tbl3.IsLocked and "🔒 CHAT LOCKED BY ADMIN"
				or "DM to " .. v6.DisplayName .. "..."

			if tbl3.Cache.DMs[arg2] then
				for _, v7 in pairs(tbl3.Cache.DMs[arg2]) do
					tbl3.RenderBubble(v7.Sender, v7.UserID, v7.Msg, v7.Time, v7.ID, v7.Edited)
				end
			end
		end

		tbl3.HandleScroll(true)
	end

	function tbl3.Send(arg1)
		if tbl3.IsLocked then
			return
		end

		local v1 = LocalPlayer.Name:lower()

		if tbl3.ReplyingTo then
			arg1 = string.format("[RPL::%s::%s::RPL] %s", tbl3.ReplyingTo.Username, tbl3.ReplyingTo.Text, arg1)
		end

		if
			(function(arg1)
				local v1 = arg1:lower()
				return v1:match("^https?://.+")
					and (
						v1:find("%.png")
						or v1:find("%.jpg")
						or v1:find("%.jpeg")
						or v1:find("%.webp")
						or v1:find("%.gif")
					)
			end)(arg1) and not arg1:match("^%[MEDIA::")
		then
			arg1 = "[MEDIA::" .. arg1 .. "::MEDIA]"
		end

		local v2 = tbl3.Mode == "DM"
		local v3 = tbl3.ActiveDM or ""
		local v4 = "M_" .. LocalPlayer.UserId .. "_" .. os.time() .. "_" .. math.random(1000, 9999)
		local v5 = v2 and func24(v1, v3) or v9() .. v10
		local v6, v7 = func22(v5, arg1)
		local v8 = func16()
		tbl3.PendingCommitGrace[v4] = os.clock()
		local v11 = HttpService:JSONEncode({ u = LocalPlayer.UserId, s = LocalPlayer.Name, m = v6, sign = v7, t = v8 })

		if v2 then
			func3("DMs/" .. v1 .. "/" .. v3 .. "/" .. v4, v11, true)
			func3("DMs/" .. v3 .. "/" .. v1 .. "/" .. v4, v11, true)
			tbl3.SetLastRead(v3, v8)
		else
			func6(LocalPlayer.Name, arg1, false, nil, false, false)
		end

		tbl3.SeenIDs[v4] = v7
		local v12 = v2 and tbl3.Cache.DMs[v3] or tbl3.Cache.Server

		if v12 then
			table.insert(v12, {
				Sender = LocalPlayer.Name,
				UserID = LocalPlayer.UserId,
				Msg = arg1,
				Time = v8,
				ID = v4,
				Edited = false,
			})
		end

		if not v2 and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
			pcall(function()
				game:GetService("Chat"):Chat(LocalPlayer.Character.Head, func26(arg1), Enum.ChatColor.White)
			end)
		end

		tbl3.RenderBubble(LocalPlayer.Name, LocalPlayer.UserId, arg1, v8, v4, false)
	end

	function tbl3.SubmitEdit(arg1, msg)
		local v1 = tbl3.Mode == "DM"
		local v2 = tbl3.ActiveDM or ""
		local v3 = LocalPlayer.Name:lower()
		local v4 = v1 and func24(v3, v2) or v9() .. v10
		local v5, v6 = func22(v4, "[EDT]" .. msg)
		local v7 = func16()

		for _, v8 in ipairs(v1 and tbl3.Cache.DMs[v2] or tbl3.Cache.Server or {}) do
			if v8.ID == arg1 then
				v8.Msg = msg
				v8.Edited = true
				break
			end
		end

		tbl3.SeenIDs[arg1] = v6
		local v11 = HttpService:JSONEncode({ u = LocalPlayer.UserId, s = LocalPlayer.Name, m = v5, sign = v6, t = v7 })

		if v1 then
			func3("DMs/" .. v3 .. "/" .. v2 .. "/" .. arg1, v11, true)
			func3("DMs/" .. v2 .. "/" .. v3 .. "/" .. arg1, v11, true)
		end

		local v12 = tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[arg1]

		if v12 and v12:FindFirstChild("MessageBubble") then
			v12.MessageBubble:SetAttribute("LiveText", msg)
			if v12.MessageBubble:FindFirstChild("MainMessageText") then
				v12.MessageBubble.MainMessageText.Text = msg
					.. "\n<font color='#888888' size='10'><i>(edited)</i></font>"
			end
		end
	end

	local str1 = ""
	local flag = false
	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(1.5) do
			if getgenv().criticalChat_Unloading then
				break
			end

			local v1 = func({
				Url = "https://axer-chat-v2-default-rtdb.asia-southeast1.firebasedatabase.app/"
					.. v4
					.. '?orderBy="$key"&limitToLast=15',
				Method = "GET",
			})

			if v1 and v1.StatusCode == 200 then
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return HttpService:JSONDecode(v1.Body)
				end)
				if ok and type(result) == "table" then
					local tbl = {}

					for k, v2 in pairs(result) do
						table.insert(tbl, { key = k, val = v2 })
					end

					table.sort(tbl, function(a, b)
						return a.key < b.key
					end)

					for _, v3 in ipairs(tbl) do
						if v3.key > str1 then
							str1 = v3.key
							if flag and v3.val.sender ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
								local sender = v3.val.sender
								local text = v3.val.text
								local v5 = v3.val.isJoin or false
								local v6 = v3.val.isAdminJoin or false
								local v7 = v3.val.timestamp or os.time()
								local v8 = "FIREBASE_" .. v3.key

								if v6 and v3.val.isSystem then
									if not tbl3.AdminJoinAlreadyDone then
										table.insert(tbl3.Cache.Server, {
											Sender = "ARIA",
											UserID = 0,
											Msg = text,
											Time = v7,
											ID = v8,
											Edited = false,
										})

										if #tbl3.Cache.Server > 100 then
											table.remove(tbl3.Cache.Server, 1)
										end

										if tbl3.Mode == "Server" and tbl3.UI.MainBg and tbl3.UI.MainBg.Visible then
											tbl3.RenderBubble("ARIA", 0, text, v7, v8, false)
										end
									end
								elseif v5 then
									local num = 0
									local v9 = Players:FindFirstChild(sender)

									if v9 then
										num = v9.UserId
									end

									table.insert(tbl3.Cache.Server, {
										Sender = sender,
										UserID = num,
										Msg = text,
										Time = v7,
										ID = v8,
										Edited = false,
									})

									if #tbl3.Cache.Server > 100 then
										table.remove(tbl3.Cache.Server, 1)
									end

									if tbl3.Mode == "Server" and tbl3.UI.MainBg and tbl3.UI.MainBg.Visible then
										tbl3.RenderBubble(sender, num, text, v7, v8, false)
									else
										tbl3.TriggerToast("👤 Player Joined", text, false, nil, sender)
									end
								elseif not v3.val.isSystem then
									local num1 = 0
									local v10 = Players:FindFirstChild(sender)

									if v10 then
										num1 = v10.UserId
									end

									table.insert(tbl3.Cache.Server, {
										Sender = sender,
										UserID = num1,
										Msg = text,
										Time = v7,
										ID = v8,
										Edited = false,
									})

									if #tbl3.Cache.Server > 100 then
										table.remove(tbl3.Cache.Server, 1)
									end

									if tbl3.Mode == "Server" and tbl3.UI.MainBg and tbl3.UI.MainBg.Visible then
										tbl3.RenderBubble(sender, num1, text, v7, v8, false)
									else
										tbl3.TriggerToast(
											"🌐 Server Chat",
											sender .. ": " .. text,
											false,
											nil,
											sender
										)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					flag = true
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	function tbl3.StartPolling()
		task.spawn(function()
			local v2 = LocalPlayer.Name:lower()
			local v3 = func1("DMs/" .. v2)

			if typeof(v3) == "table" then
				for k, v4 in pairs(v3) do
					if typeof(v4) == "table" then
						tbl3.Cache.DMs[k] = tbl3.Cache.DMs[k] or {}
						local v5 = func24(v2, k)
						local tbl = {}

						for k1, v6 in pairs(v4) do
							if k1 ~= "_typ" and typeof(v6) == "table" and tonumber(v6.t) then
								if func16() - tonumber(v6.t) > 604800 then
									task.spawn(function()
										func4("DMs/" .. v2 .. "/" .. k .. "/" .. k1)
									end)
								else
									tbl3.SeenIDs[k1] = v6.sign

									if v6.s and v6.u then
										tbl3.RegisterAccount(v6.s, nil, v6.u)
									end

									local v7 = func23(v5, v6.m, v6.sign)

									if v7 then
										table.insert(tbl, {
											Sender = v6.s,
											UserID = v6.u,
											Msg = v7:sub(1, 5) == "[EDT]" and v7:sub(6) or v7,
											Time = tonumber(v6.t),
											ID = k1,
											Edited = v7:sub(1, 5) == "[EDT]",
										})
									end
								end
							end
						end

						table.sort(tbl, function(a, b)
							return a.Time < b.Time
						end)

						for _, v8 in ipairs(tbl) do
							table.insert(tbl3.Cache.DMs[k], v8)
						end
					end
				end
			end

			while task.wait(1.5) do
				if not getgenv().criticalChat_Unloading then
					if screenGui and screenGui.Parent then
						local v9 = func16()
						local v10 = tbl3.UI.MainBg and tbl3.UI.MainBg.Visible
						local v11 = func1("DMs/" .. v2)

						if v11 then
							for k2, v12 in pairs(v11) do
								tbl3.Cache.DMs[k2] = tbl3.Cache.DMs[k2] or {}
								local v13 = func24(v2, k2)
								local tbl1 = {}

								if not tbl3.AccountRegistry[k2] then
									task.spawn(function()
										local UserIdFromNameAsync = Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(k2)
										local k2Ref = k2

										if v1 and UserIdFromNameAsync then
											pcall(function()
												k2Ref =
													v1:GetUserInfosByUserIdsAsync({ tonumber(UserIdFromNameAsync) })[1].DisplayName
											end)
										end

										tbl3.RegisterAccount(k2, k2Ref, UserIdFromNameAsync)
									end)
								end

								for k3, v14 in pairs(v12) do
									tbl1[k3] = true
									if k3 ~= "_typ" then
										local num = tonumber(typeof(v14) == "table" and v14.t or 0)
										if v9 - num > 604800 then
											task.spawn(function()
												func4("DMs/" .. v2 .. "/" .. k2 .. "/" .. k3)
												func4("DMs/" .. k2 .. "/" .. v2 .. "/" .. k3)
											end)
											if tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3] then
												tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3]:Destroy()
												tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3] = nil
											end
										elseif
											typeof(v14) == "table"
											and tbl3.SeenIDs[k3] ~= v14.sign
											and v14.m
											and v14.sign
										then
											local v15 = tbl3.SeenIDs[k3] ~= nil
											tbl3.SeenIDs[k3] = v14.sign

											if v14.s and v14.u then
												tbl3.RegisterAccount(v14.s, nil, v14.u)
											end

											local v16 = func23(v13, v14.m, v14.sign)

											if v16 then
												local v17 = v16:sub(1, 5) == "[EDT]"
												local v18 = v17 and v16:sub(6) or v16
												local flag = false

												for k4 in pairs(tbl3.MutedKeywords) do
													if string.find(string.lower(v18), k4) then
														flag = true
														break
													end
												end

												if not (flag and v14.s:lower() ~= LocalPlayer.Name:lower()) then
													if v15 then
														for _, v19 in ipairs(tbl3.Cache.DMs[k2]) do
															if v19.ID == k3 then
																v19.Msg = v18
																v19.Edited = true
																break
															end
														end
														if
															tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3]
															and tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3]:FindFirstChild(
																"MessageBubble"
															)
														then
															tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3].MessageBubble:SetAttribute(
																"LiveText",
																v18
															)
															if
																tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3].MessageBubble:FindFirstChild(
																	"MainMessageText"
																)
															then
																tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k3].MessageBubble.MainMessageText.Text = v18
																	.. "\n<font color='#888888' size='10'><i>(edited)</i></font>"
															end
														end
													else
														table.insert(tbl3.Cache.DMs[k2], {
															Sender = v14.s,
															UserID = v14.u,
															Msg = v18,
															Time = num,
															ID = k3,
															Edited = v17,
														})
														if v10 and tbl3.Mode == "DM" and tbl3.ActiveDM == k2 then
															tbl3.RenderBubble(v14.s, v14.u, v18, num, k3, v17)
															tbl3.SetLastRead(k2, (func16()))
														else
															tbl3.TriggerToast(
																"💬 DM : " .. tbl3.GetAccount(k2).DisplayName,
																v18,
																true,
																k2,
																v14.s
															)
														end
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end

								if tbl3.Mode == "DM" and tbl3.ActiveDM == k2 then
									for k5, activeBubbleNode in pairs(tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes) do
										if not tbl1[k5] and k5 ~= "_typ" then
											if
												not (
													tbl3.PendingCommitGrace[k5]
													and os.clock() - tbl3.PendingCommitGrace[k5] < 45
												)
											then
												activeBubbleNode:Destroy()
												tbl3.ActiveBubbleNodes[k5] = nil
											end
										elseif tbl1[k5] then
											tbl3.PendingCommitGrace[k5] = nil
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						tbl3.LiveUpdateInboxSort()
						func15()
						continue
					end
				end
				break
			end
		end)
	end

	function tbl3.UpdateThemeAnimated()
		local v1 = tbl4[tbl5[num]]
		local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		TweenService
			:Create(tbl3.UI.MainBg, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = v1.MainBg, BackgroundTransparency = v1.BgTrans })
			:Play()
		TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.MainStroke, tweenInfo, { Color = v1.StrokeColor }):Play()
		TweenService
			:Create(
				tbl3.UI.HeaderBg,
				tweenInfo,
				{ BackgroundColor3 = v1.HeaderBg, BackgroundTransparency = v1.HeadTrans }
			)
			:Play()
		TweenService:Create(
			tbl3.UI.InputWrapper,
			tweenInfo,
			{ BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg, BackgroundTransparency = v1.InpTrans }
		):Play()
		TweenService:Create(
			tbl3.UI.SearchWrapper,
			tweenInfo,
			{ BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg, BackgroundTransparency = v1.InpTrans }
		):Play()
		TweenService:Create(
			tbl3.UI.ReplyPreview,
			tweenInfo,
			{ BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg, BackgroundTransparency = v1.InpTrans }
		):Play()

		if tbl3.UI.StickerTray then
			TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.StickerTray, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = v1.HeaderBg }):Play()
			if tbl3.UI.StickerTray:FindFirstChild("UIStroke") then
				TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.StickerTray.UIStroke, tweenInfo, { Color = v1.Accent }):Play()
			end
		end

		if tbl3.UI.ImageTray then
			TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.ImageTray, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = v1.HeaderBg }):Play()
			if tbl3.UI.ImageTray:FindFirstChild("UIStroke") then
				TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.ImageTray.UIStroke, tweenInfo, { Color = v1.Accent }):Play()
			end
		end

		tbl3.UI.Title.TextColor3 = v1.TextColor
		tbl3.UI.TextBox.TextColor3 = tbl3.IsLocked and Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80) or v1.TextColor
		tbl3.UI.TextBox.PlaceholderColor3 = v1.SubText
		tbl3.UI.SearchInput.TextColor3 = v1.TextColor
		tbl3.UI.SearchInput.PlaceholderColor3 = v1.SubText
		tbl3.UI.ReplyText.TextColor3 = v1.SubText
		tbl3.UI.CancelRep.TextColor3 = v1.SubText
		tbl3.UI.ThemeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg
		tbl3.UI.InboxBtn.BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg
		tbl3.UI.MuteBtn.BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg
		tbl3.UI.StickerBtn.BackgroundColor3 = v1.HeaderBg
		tbl3.UI.ImageBtn.BackgroundColor3 = v1.HeaderBg
		tbl3.UI.SendBtn.BackgroundColor3 = tbl3.IsLocked and Color3.fromRGB(80, 20, 20) or v1.Accent

		if tbl3.UI.AdminBtn then
			TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.AdminBtn, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg }):Play()
		end

		if tbl3.UI.AdminTray then
			TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.AdminTray, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = v1.HeaderBg }):Play()
			TweenService:Create(tbl3.UI.AdminSearch, tweenInfo, {
				BackgroundColor3 = v1.InputBg,
				TextColor3 = v1.TextColor,
				PlaceholderColor3 = v1.SubText,
			}):Play()
		end

		for _, child in pairs(tbl3.UI.Scroll:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Frame") and child:FindFirstChild("MessageBubble") then
				local MessageBubble = child.MessageBubble
				local v2 = MessageBubble.AnchorPoint.X == 1
				local v3 = v2 and v1.MyBubble or v1.OtherBubble
				local v4 = v2 and v1.BubMeTrans or v1.BubOtherTrans

				if MessageBubble:GetAttribute("IsVIPBubble") then
					func25(MessageBubble, tbl3.CurrentTheme)
				else
					TweenService
						:Create(MessageBubble, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = v3, BackgroundTransparency = v4 })
						:Play()

					if MessageBubble:FindFirstChild("MainMessageText") then
						MessageBubble.MainMessageText.TextColor3 = v1.TextColor
					end

					if MessageBubble:FindFirstChild("SenderName") and MessageBubble.SenderName.Text ~= "ARIA" then
						MessageBubble.SenderName.TextColor3 = v1.NameColor
					end
				end

				if MessageBubble:GetAttribute("IsMediaOnly") then
					MessageBubble.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				end
			elseif child.Name:match("^DM_Card_") or child.Name:match("^SearchCard_") then
				if child:FindFirstChild("criticalUnreadBadge") then
					TweenService:Create(child, tweenInfo, { BackgroundColor3 = v1.Accent }):Play()
				else
					TweenService:Create(child, tweenInfo, {
						BackgroundColor3 = v1.OtherBubble,
						BackgroundTransparency = v1.BubOtherTrans,
					}):Play()
				end

				if child:FindFirstChild("CardName") then
					child.CardName.TextColor3 = v1.TextColor
				end

				if child:FindFirstChild("Sub") then
					child.Sub.TextColor3 = v1.SubText
				end
			end
		end

		tbl3.SyncNativeRobloxBubbleSettings()
		tbl3.LiveSyncAllVisibleBubblesTitle()
	end

	tbl3.BuildMasterUI()
	tbl3.StartPolling()
	func34()
	task.spawn(function()
		local v1 = func12()
		local v2 = "Welcome dear " .. LocalPlayer.DisplayName .. "!"
		local v3 = v1 and "👑 critical Chat's Creator has entered the server!\nSIR critical HAS ENTERED!" or v2
		local v4 = "SYS_LOCAL_ARIA_" .. LocalPlayer.UserId
		table.insert(
			tbl3.Cache.Server,
			{ Sender = "ARIA", UserID = 0, Msg = v3, Time = os.time(), ID = v4, Edited = false }
		)

		if tbl3.Mode == "Server" and tbl3.UI.MainBg.Visible then
			tbl3.RenderBubble("ARIA", 0, v3, os.time(), v4, false)
		end

		tbl3.TriggerArrivalToast(LocalPlayer.DisplayName)

		if v1 and not tbl3.AdminJoinAlreadyDone then
			func7()
			task.wait(1)
			func13()
		else
			func8()
		end

		task.wait(0.5)
		func2("Srv/" .. v10 .. "/SYS_NET_ARRIVAL_" .. LocalPlayer.UserId .. "_" .. os.time(), {
			u = LocalPlayer.UserId,
			s = "SYS_ARIA_ARRIVAL",
			m = LocalPlayer.DisplayName,
			sign = "ARR_SECURE_" .. os.time(),
			t = os.time(),
		})
	end)
	local flag1 = false
	local v19
	local v20
	local Position

	local function func41(arg1)
		if not arg1 or arg1 == "" then
			return nil
		end

		local v1 = arg1:lower()

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if player.Name:lower() == v1 or player.DisplayName:lower() == v1 then
				return player
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function func42(character)
		if not character then
			return nil
		end

		local HumanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if HumanoidRootPart then
			return HumanoidRootPart
		end

		local Head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")

		if Head then
			return Head
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function func43(arg1)
		if not arg1 then
			return nil
		end
		return arg1:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	end

	local function func44()
		if tbl3.IsLocked then
			return
		end

		local Text = tbl3.UI.TextBox.Text

		if Text ~= "" then
			if string.len(Text) > 200 then
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal(
					"⚠️ <b>Limit Exceeded!</b>\nYour message contains over 200 alphabets/characters. Keep it short!"
				)
				return
			end

			if string.lower(Text) == string.lower(tbl3.LastSentMessage) then
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("⚠️ <b>Spam Alert!</b>\nYou cannot send the exact same message repeatedly.")
				return
			end

			local v1 = os.clock()
			local tbl = {}

			for _, messageTimestamp in ipairs(tbl3.MessageTimestamps) do
				if v1 - messageTimestamp <= 3 then
					table.insert(tbl, messageTimestamp)
				end
			end

			tbl3.MessageTimestamps = tbl

			if #tbl3.MessageTimestamps >= 5 then
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal(
					"⚠️ <b>Spam Blocked!</b>\nYou are typing too fast! (Limit: 5 messages per 3 seconds)"
				)
				return
			end

			tbl3.UI.TextBox.Text = ""
			tbl3.LastSentMessage = Text
			table.insert(tbl3.MessageTimestamps, os.clock())

			if Text:lower():match("^/time$") or Text:lower():match("^/time ") then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal(
					"🕐 **Your Current Time:**\n" .. os.date("%A, %B %d, %Y") .. "\n" .. os.date("%I:%M:%S %p")
				)
				return
			end

			local v2 = Text:lower()

			if v2:match("^/help$") or v2:match("^/help ") then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				local str

				if func12() then
					str =
						"📚 <b>Available Commands:</b>\n\n<b>📌 Utility:</b>\n/help, /time, /helpphoto, /respawn\n\n<b>✈️ Movement:</b>\n/fly, /to &lt;player&gt;, /back\n\n<b>👁️ Camera:</b>\n/view &lt;player&gt;, /unview\n\n<b>👑 Admin:</b>\n/announce, /vip, /unvip, /title, /untitle"
				else
					str =
						"📚 <b>Available Commands:</b>\n\n<b>📌 Utility:</b>\n/help, /time, /helpphoto, /respawn\n\n<b>✈️ Movement:</b>\n/fly, /to &lt;player&gt;, /back\n\n<b>👁️ Camera:</b>\n/view &lt;player&gt;, /unview\n\n<font color='#B43CFF'>Admin commands hidden.</font>"
				end

				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal(str)
				return
			end

			local v3 = Text:match("^/announce%s+(.+)$")

			if v3 then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()

				if func12() then
					func33(v3)
				else
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ <b>Access Denied</b>\nOnly admins can send cross-server announcements.")
				end

				return
			end

			if v2:match("^/respawn$") then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

				if Character then
					local v4 = func43(Character)
					if v4 then
						v4.Health = 0
						tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("💀 You have been respawned.")
					else
						tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ No humanoid found.")
					end
				else
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ You have no character.")
				end

				return
			end

			if v2:match("^/fly$") then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()

				if flag1 then
					flag1 = false

					if v19 then
						v19:Disconnect()
						v19 = nil
					end

					if v20 then
						v20:Destroy()
						v20 = nil
					end

					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("🛑 Flight disabled.")
				else
					flag1 = true
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("✈️ Flight enabled! Type /fly again to disable.")

					if v19 then
						v19:Disconnect()
					end

					v19 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
						local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

						if not Character or not flag1 then
							flag1 = false

							if v19 then
								v19:Disconnect()
								v19 = nil
							end

							return
						end

						local v1 = func42(Character)

						if not v1 then
							return
						end

						local v2 = func43(Character)

						if not v2 then
							return
						end

						v2.PlatformStand = true

						if not v20 or not v20.Parent then
							v20 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							local v3 = v20
							v3.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 4000, 0)
							v3.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
							v3.Parent = v1
						end

						v20.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
					end)
				end

				return
			end

			local v5 = v2:match("^/view%s+(.+)$")

			if v5 then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				local v6 = func41(v5)

				if not v6 or v6 == LocalPlayer then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ Player not found.")
					return
				end

				local Character1 = v6.Character

				if not Character1 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ That player has no character.")
					return
				end

				local v7 = func42(Character1)

				if not v7 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ Cannot find part.")
					return
				end

				workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = v7
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("👀 Now viewing " .. v6.DisplayName .. " (@" .. v6.Name .. ").")
				return
			end

			if v2:match("^/unview$") then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character

				if Character2 then
					local v8 = func42(Character2)
					if v8 then
						workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = v8
						tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("👁️ Camera returned.")
					else
						tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ Cannot find your root.")
					end
				else
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ You have no character.")
				end

				return
			end

			local v9 = v2:match("^/to%s+(.+)$")

			if v9 then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				local v10 = func41(v9)

				if not v10 or v10 == LocalPlayer then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ Player not found.")
					return
				end

				local Character3 = LocalPlayer.Character

				if not Character3 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ You have no character.")
					return
				end

				local v11 = func42(Character3)

				if not v11 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ Cannot find your root.")
					return
				end

				local Character4 = v10.Character

				if not Character4 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ No target char.")
					return
				end

				local v12 = func42(Character4)

				if not v12 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ Cannot find their root.")
					return
				end

				Position = v11.Position
				v11.CFrame = v12.CFrame
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("🚀 Teleported to " .. v10.DisplayName .. ".")
				return
			end

			if v2:match("^/back$") then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()

				if not Position then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ No previous location stored.")
					return
				end

				local Character5 = LocalPlayer.Character

				if not Character5 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ You have no character.")
					return
				end

				local v13 = func42(Character5)

				if not v13 then
					tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("❌ Cannot find your root.")
					return
				end

				v13.Position = Position
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal("🔙 Returned to previous location.")
				Position = nil
				return
			end

			if Text:lower():match("^/helpphoto") then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				tbl3.TriggerAriaLocal(
					"📸 <b>ARIA Media Guide:</b>\n\n1. Copy any image link.\n2. Click the <b>🖼️ Image Button</b>.\n3. Paste link and click <b>Save</b>.\n4. Tap saved tile to broadcast!\n5. ⭐ Star to favorite!\n6. Use tabs All/Favorites"
				)
				return
			end

			local v14 = Text:match("^/vip%s+(.+)$")
			local v15 = Text:match("^/unvip%s+(.+)$")
			local v16, v17 = Text:match("^/title%s+(@?%S+)%s+(.+)$")
			local v18 = Text:match("^/untitle%s+(@?%S+)$")

			if v14 or v15 or v16 or v18 then
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()

				if func12() then
					local v21 = v16 and "GRANT_TITLE:" .. v16:gsub("^@", ""):lower() .. ":" .. v17
						or (
							v18 and "REVOKE_TITLE:" .. v18:gsub("^@", ""):lower()
							or (v14 and "GRANT_VIP:" .. v14 or "REVOKE_VIP:" .. v15)
						)
					tbl3.DispatchGodCommand(v21)
				else
					func11("ADM-SPOOF", "Admin Protocol Breach")
				end

				return
			end

			if tbl3.EditingMsgID then
				local EditingMsgID = tbl3.EditingMsgID
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
				tbl3.SubmitEdit(EditingMsgID, Text)
			else
				tbl3.Send(Text)
				tbl3.CancelActionBar()
			end
		end
	end

	tbl3.UI.SendBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(func44)
	tbl3.UI.TextBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		if enterPressed then
			func44()
		end
	end)
	tbl3.UI.CancelRep.MouseButton1Click:Connect(tbl3.CancelActionBar)
	local v21
	tbl3.UI.SearchInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		if tbl3.Mode ~= "DM_Inbox" then
			return
		end

		local v2 = tbl3.UI.SearchInput.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")

		for _, child in pairs(tbl3.UI.Scroll:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end

		if not v2 or #v2 < 1 then
			tbl3.LiveUpdateInboxSort()
			return
		end

		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", tbl3.UI.Scroll)
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 35)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
		textLabel.TextSize = 11
		textLabel.TextColor3 = tbl4[tbl3.CurrentTheme].SubText
		textLabel.Text = "🔍 Searching '" .. v2 .. "'..."

		if v21 then
			task.cancel(v21)
		end

		v21 = task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(0.25)
			local num = 0
			local tbl = {}

			for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if
					player ~= LocalPlayer
					and (player.Name:lower():find(v2:lower()) or player.DisplayName:lower():find(v2:lower()))
				then
					tbl[player.Name:lower()] = true
					num = num + 1
					tbl3.RegisterAccount(player.Name, player.DisplayName, player.UserId)
					tbl3.RenderUserSearchCard(player.DisplayName, player.Name, player.UserId)
				end
			end

			if #v2 >= 3 then
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(v2)
				end)
				if ok and result then
					local v3 = v2
					local v4 = v3

					if v1 then
						pcall(function()
							local UserInfosByUserIdsAsync = v1:GetUserInfosByUserIdsAsync({ result })
							v3 = UserInfosByUserIdsAsync[1].Username or v2
							v4 = UserInfosByUserIdsAsync[1].DisplayName or v3
						end)
					else
						pcall(function()
							v3 = Players:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(result)
							v4 = v3
						end)
					end

					if v3 ~= LocalPlayer.Name and not tbl[v3:lower()] then
						num = num + 1
						tbl3.RegisterAccount(v3, v4, result)
						tbl3.RenderUserSearchCard(v4, v3, result)
					end
				end
			end

			if textLabel and textLabel.Parent then
				textLabel:Destroy()
			end

			if num == 0 then
				local textLabel1 = Instance.new("TextLabel", tbl3.UI.Scroll)
				textLabel1.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
				textLabel1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel1.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
				textLabel1.TextSize = 12
				textLabel1.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 70, 70)
				textLabel1.Text = "No users found matching '" .. v2 .. "'"
			end
		end)
	end)
	tbl3.UI.Back.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if tbl3.Mode == "DM" then
			tbl3.SwitchMode("DM_Inbox")
		elseif tbl3.Mode == "DM_Inbox" then
			tbl3.SwitchMode("Server")
		end
	end)
	tbl3.UI.InboxBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		tbl3.SwitchMode("DM_Inbox")
	end)
	tbl3.UI.MuteBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		tbl3.MuteServerToasts = not tbl3.MuteServerToasts
		tbl3.UI.MuteBtn.Text = tbl3.MuteServerToasts and "🔕" or "🔊"
		tbl3.TriggerToast("🔕 Alerts", tbl3.MuteServerToasts and "Popups Muted" or "Popups Active", false)
	end)
	tbl3.UI.ThemeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		num = num % 3 + 1
		tbl3.CurrentTheme = tbl5[num]
		tbl3.UI.ThemeBtn.Text = tbl6[tbl3.CurrentTheme]
		tbl3.UpdateThemeAnimated()
	end)
end
